Defaults to None.\n        """\n        instruction = f"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`."\n        # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna otherwise asks for clarification and never edits.\n        instruction += (\n            f"\\n\\nThe repository\'s README.md describes the goal. run_harness_polyglot(root_dir, output_dir, genid, skip_staged_eval=False, num_samples=-1):\n    # NOTE: the harness for polyglot is different because each task instance needs a docker container\n    from domains.polyglot.harness import harness as harness_polyglot\n    from domains.polyglot.report import report as report_polyglot\n\n    eval_output_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"gen_{genid}", model_patch_paths=patch_files,\n            num_evals=1,\n            num_evals_parallel=1,\n            pred_dname=eval_output_dir,\n            output_dir=eval_output_dir,\n genid, {"run_full_eval": run_next_eval})\n\ndef select_next_parent_container(\n    docker_client,\n    domains,\n    generate_output_dir,\n    archive,\n    root_dir="./",\n    root_commit="HEAD",\n get_latest_can_select_parent(archive, generate_output_dir)\n    safe_log(f"select_next_parent_container: latest_node={latest_node}")\n    prev_patch_files = get_patch_files(generate_output_dir, "/tmp/"\n\n    try:\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files, verbose=False)\n\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\n generate_output_dir.split(os.sep)[-1]\n        )\n        copy_to_container(\n            container,\n            source_path=generate_output_dir,\n "timeout",\n                "10800",  # 3h timeout\n                "python",\n                "-m",\n                "utils.run_ensemble",\n                "--domain",\n                domain,\n                "--generate_output_dir",\n                container_generate_output_dir,\n                "--num_samples",\n                str(num_samples),\n                "--max_workers",\n                str(max_workers),\n                "--subset",\n                subset,\n            ]\n            exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\n            log_container_output(exec_result)\n\n            # Get ensemble outputs\n            container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\n")\n            score = float(container_output_strings[-3])\n            scores.append(score)\n--- task_agent.py ---\nimport json\nimport os\nimport re\nimport socket\nimport http.client\nimport time\n\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\n\n\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\n        self._path = path\n\n    def connect(self):\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\n        s.connect(self._path)\n        self.sock = s\n\n\ndef call_model(prompt):\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\n    last = None\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\n            r = c.getresponse()\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\n            c.close()\n            if r.status == 200:\n                return data.get("text", "")\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\'error\')}"\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\n\n\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\n    return "\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\n\n\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\n    parts = [\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input. "\n        "Use every demonstration to check the rule: mentally apply it to each example and reject any "\n        "interpretation that fails to reproduce an example exactly.",\n        "",\n    ]\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\n        parts.append("")\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\n    parts.append("## Test")\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\n    parts.append("")\n    parts.append(\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\n"\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\n    )\n    return "\\n".join(parts)\n\n\ndef build_review_prompt(inputs, previous_candidate=None):\n    """Build an unanchored second solve from the demonstrations alone."""\n    return (\n        build_prompt(inputs)\n        + "\\n\\n## Independent second attempt\\n"\n        + "Solve this puzzle from scratch using only the training input/output pairs and "\n        + "the test input above. Do not assume any earlier proposed answer is correct. "\n        + "As a fresh check, identify the objects and their colors and positions, compare "\n        + "their changes across every example, and verify the inferred rule against each "\n        + "pair before applying it to the test. Check output dimensions, boundaries, and "\n        + "unchanged cells. Return your best complete grid inside <answer></answer> tags, "\n        + "with valid JSON rows."\n    )\n\n\ndef _valid(g):\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\n\n\ndef _parse_block(s):\n    s = s.strip()\n    try:\n        g = json.loads(s)\n        if _valid(g):\n            return g\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\n    rows = []\n    for line in s.splitlines():\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\n        if not line:\n            continue\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\d\\s,]+", line) else None\n        if not toks:\n            return None\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\n\n\ndef parse_grid(text):\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\n    if not text:\n        return None\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\[\\s*\\[[\\d\\s,\\[\\]]*\\]\\s*\\]", text)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    return None\n\n\nclass TaskAgent:\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\n        self.model = model\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\n\n    def log(self, msg):\n        try:\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\n                f.write(msg + "\\n\\n")\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    def solve_once(self, inputs, independent=False):\n        """One model response -> one parsed grid (or None)."""\n        prompt = build_review_prompt(inputs) if independent else build_prompt(inputs)\n        self.log(f"Input: {prompt}")\n        text = call_model(prompt)\n        self.log(f"Output: {text}")\n        return parse_grid(text)\n\n    def forward(self, inputs):\n        """\n        Args:\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\n        """\n        answers = []\n        # Preserve the first candidate; spend the second call on a fresh derivation rather\n        # than repeating the same prompt or anchoring the model on its first guess.\n        try:\n            first = self.solve_once(inputs)\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.log(f"Error: {e}")\n            first = None\n        if first is not None:\n            answers.append(first)\n\n        try:\n            second = self.solve_once(inputs, independent=True)\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.log(f"Error: {e}")\n            second = None\n        if second is not None and second not in answers:\n            answers.append(second)\n        return answers, []\n--- results summary ---\ngen_1 {\'overall_accuracy\': 0.78, \'total\': os\nimport re\nimport socket\nimport http.client\nimport time\n\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\n\n\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\n        self._path = path\n\n    def connect(self):\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\n        s.connect(self._path)\n        self.sock = s\n\n\ndef call_model(prompt):\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\n    last = None\n    for attempt in range(3):\n        try:\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\n            r = c.getresponse()\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\n            c.close()\n            if r.status == 200:\n                return data.get("text", "")\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\'error\')}"\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\n                break\n        except Exception as e:\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\n\n\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\n    return "\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\n\n\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\n    parts = [\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input. "\n        "Use every demonstration to check the rule: mentally apply it to each example and reject any "\n        "interpretation that fails to reproduce an example exactly.",\n        "",\n    ]\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\n        parts.append("")\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\n    parts.append("## Test")\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\n    parts.append("")\n    parts.append(\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\n"\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\n    )\n    return "\\n".join(parts)\n\n\ndef build_review_prompt(inputs, previous_candidate=None):\n    """Build an unanchored second solve from the demonstrations alone."""\n    return (\n        build_prompt(inputs)\n        + "\\n\\n## Independent second attempt\\n"\n        + "Solve this puzzle from scratch using only the training input/output pairs and "\n        + "the test input above. Do not assume any earlier proposed answer is correct. "\n        + "As a fresh check, identify the objects and their colors and positions, compare "\n        + "their changes across every example, and verify the inferred rule against each "\n        + "pair before applying it to the test. Check output dimensions, boundaries, and "\n        + "unchanged cells. Return your best complete grid inside <answer></answer> tags, "\n        + "with valid JSON rows."\n    )\n\n\ndef _valid(g):\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\n\n\ndef _parse_block(s):\n    s = s.strip()\n    try:\n        g = json.loads(s)\n        if _valid(g):\n            return g\n    except Exception:\n        pass\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\n    rows = []\n    for line in s.splitlines():\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\n        if not line:\n            continue\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\d\\s,]+", line) else None\n        if not toks:\n            return None\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\n\n\ndef parse_grid(text):\n    """Extract the answer grid from a model response; None if no valid grid is found."""\n    if not text:\n        return None\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"<answer>(.*?)</answer>", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"```(?:json)?\\s*(.*?)```", text, re.DOTALL)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    for m in reversed(re.findall(r"\\[\\s*\\[[\\d\\s,\\[\\]]*\\]\\s*\\]", text)):\n        g = _parse_block(m)\n        if g is not None:\n            return g\n    return None\n\n\nclass TaskAgent:\n    def __init__(self, model="gpt-6-luna", chat_history_file="./chat_history.md"):\n        self.model = model\n        self.chat_history_file = chat_history_file\n\n    def log(self, msg):\n        try:\n            with open(self.chat_history_file, "a") as f:\n                f.write(msg + "\\n\\n")\n        except Exception:\n            pass\n\n    def solve_once(self, inputs, independent=False):\n        """One model response -> one parsed grid (or None)."""\n        prompt = build_review_prompt(inputs) if independent else build_prompt(inputs)\n        self.log(f"Input: {prompt}")\n        text = call_model(prompt)\n        self.log(f"Output: {text}")\n        return parse_grid(text)\n\n    def forward(self, inputs):\n        """\n        Args:\n            inputs (dict): {"domain": "arc", "train": [{"input": grid, "output": grid}, ...], "test_input": grid}\n        Returns:\n            tuple: (prediction, msg_history) where prediction is a list of up to 2 candidate grids.\n        """\n        answers = []\n        # Preserve the first candidate; spend the second call on a fresh derivation rather\n        # than repeating the same prompt or anchoring the model on its first guess.\n        try:\n            first = self.solve_once(inputs)\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.log(f"Error: {e}")\n            first = None\n        if first is not None:\n            answers.append(first)\n\n        try:\n            second = self.solve_once(inputs, independent=True)\n        except Exception as e:\n            self.log(f"Error: {e}")\n            second = None\n        if second is not None and second not in answers:\n            answers.append(second)\n        return answers, []\n--- best generation details ---\n{\n    "gen_output_dir": "/home/user/HyperAgents/exp/runs/source_s2/generate_source_s2/gen_9",\n    "current_genid": 9,\n    "parent_genid": 5,\n    "run_baseline": null,\n    "prev_patch_files": [\n        "/home/user/HyperAgents/exp/runs/source_s2/generate_source_s2/gen_1/agent_output/model_patch.diff",\n        "/home/user/HyperAgents/exp/runs/source_s2/generate_source_s2/gen_2/agent_output/model_patch.diff",\n        "/home/user/HyperAgents/exp/runs/source_s2/generate_source_s2/gen_4/agent_output/model_patch.diff",\n        "/home/user/HyperAgents/exp/runs/source_s2/generate_source_s2/gen_5/agent_output/model_patch.diff"\n    ],\n    "curr_patch_files": [\n        "/home/user/HyperAgents/exp/runs/source_s2/generate_source_s2/gen_9/agent_output/model_patch.diff"\n    ],\n    "parent_agent_success": true,\n    "optimize_option": "only_agent",\n    "agent_archive_path": null,\n    "can_select_next_parent": true,\n    "run_eval": true,\n    "run_full_eval": true,\n    "valid_parent": true\n}Input: "Modify any part of the codebase at `/hyperagents/`.\\n\\nThe repository\'s README.md describes the goal. chat_with_agent\\n\\n\\ndef summarize_evaluation_history(eval_path, max_reports=12):\\n    """Summarize prior generation reports for the meta-agent prompt.\\n\\n    Reports are grouped by generation and evaluation type (for example, arc_eval).\\n    Malformed files and reports without accuracy scores are ignored so an incomplete\\n    history cannot prevent an improvement run.\\n    """\\n    if not eval_path or not os.path.isdir(eval_path):\\n        return "No evaluation reports were found (evaluation directory is unavailable)."\\n\\n    reports = []\\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(eval_path):\\n        dirs.sort()\\n        if "report.json" not in files:\\n            continue\\n        relative_parts = os.path.relpath(root, eval_path).split(os.sep)\\n        generation = None\\n        generation_index = None\\n        for index, part in enumerate(relative_parts):\\n            match = re.fullmatch(r"gen_(initial|\\\\d+)(?:\\\\..*)?", part)\\n            if match:\\n                label = match.group(1)\\n                generation = -1 if label == "initial" else int(label)\\n                generation_index = index\\n                break\\n        if generation is None:\\n            continue\\n        try:\\n            with open(os.path.join(root, "report.json"), encoding="utf-8") as stream:\\n                data = json.load(stream)\\n        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\\n            continue\\n        if not isinstance(data, dict):\\n            continue\\n        score = data.get("overall_accuracy", data.get("accuracy"))\\n        try:\\n            score = float(score)\\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\\n            continue\\n        kind_parts = relative_parts[generation_index + 1:]\\n        kind = "/".join(kind_parts) if kind_parts else "report"\\n        task_scores = data.get("per_task_score", {})\\n        failed = sorted(\\n            str(task) for task, value in task_scores.items()\\n            if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value < 1\\n        ) if isinstance(task_scores, dict) else []\\n        reports.append((generation, label, kind, score, data.get("total"), failed))\\n\\n    if not reports:\\n        return "No usable evaluation reports were found in the supplied history."\\n\\n    reports.sort(key=lambda row: (row[0], row[2]))\\n    lines = ["Prior evaluation results (accuracy; lower-scoring generations are useful regression evidence):"]\\n    # Keep the newest records while maintaining chronological order.\\n    for _, generation_label, kind, score, total, failed in reports[-max_reports:]:\\n        count = f", {total} tasks" if isinstance(total, int) else ""\\n        failures = f"; partial/failed task IDs: {\\\', \\\'.join(failed[:12])}" if failed else "; no partial/failed tasks reported"\\n        lines.append(f"- gen_{generation_label} [{kind}]: {score:.3f}{count}{failures}")\\n\\n    by_kind = {}\\n    for report in reports:\\n        by_kind.setdefault(report[2], []).append(report)\\n    for kind, entries in sorted(by_kind.items()):\\n        if len(entries) < 2:\\n            continue\\n        best = max(entries, key=lambda row: row[3])\\n        latest = entries[-1]\\n        if latest[3] < best[3]:\\n            lines.append(\\n                f"- Regression signal for {kind}: latest gen_{latest[1]} is {latest[3]:.3f}, "\\n recursively self-improves.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            repo_path (str): The path to the repository.\\n            eval_path (str): The path to previously generated agents and their evaluation results.\\n            iterations_left (int, optional): The number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future. Defaults to None.\\n        """\\n        instruction = f"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`."\\n        # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna otherwise asks for clarification and never edits.\\n        evaluation_summary = summarize_evaluation_history(eval_path)\\n        instruction += (\\n            f"\\\\n\\\\nThe repository\\\'s README.md describes the goal. new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(instruction, model=self.model, msg_history=[], logging=self.log, tools_available=\\\'all\\\')\\n--- task agent ---\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport socket\\nimport http.client\\nimport time\\n\\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\\n\\n\\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\\n        self._path = path\\n\\n    def connect(self):\\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\\n        s.connect(self._path)\\n        self.sock = s\\n\\n\\ndef call_model(prompt):\\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\\n    last = None\\n    for attempt in range(3):\\n        try:\\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\\n            r = c.getresponse()\\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\\n            c.close()\\n            if r.status == 200:\\n                return data.get("text", "")\\n            last = f"gateway HTTP {r.status}: {data.get(\\\'error\\\')}"\\n            if r.status in (402, 403, 429):\\n                break\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\\n\\n\\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\\n    return "\\\\n".join(" ".join(str(v) for v in row) for row in grid)\\n\\n\\ndef build_prompt(inputs):\\n    parts = [\\n        "You are solving an ARC-AGI puzzle. Each grid is a rectangular 2D array of integers 0-9 "\\n        "(each integer is a color). The demonstration pairs below share one hidden transformation rule "\\n        "that maps each input grid to its output grid. Infer the rule and apply it to the test input. "\\n        "Use every demonstration to check the rule: mentally apply it to each example and reject any "\\n        "interpretation that fails to reproduce an example exactly.",\\n        "",\\n    ]\\n    for i, pair in enumerate(inputs["train"], 1):\\n        parts.append(f"## Example {i}")\\n        h, w = len(pair["input"]), len(pair["input"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Input ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["input"]))\\n        h, w = len(pair["output"]), len(pair["output"][0])\\n        parts.append(f"Output ({h}x{w}):")\\n        parts.append(grid_to_text(pair["output"]))\\n        parts.append("")\\n    t = inputs["test_input"]\\n    parts.append("## Test")\\n    parts.append(f"Input ({len(t)}x{len(t[0])}):")\\n    parts.append(grid_to_text(t))\\n    parts.append("")\\n    parts.append(\\n        "Give the output grid for the test input. End your response with the grid inside <answer></answer> "\\n        "tags as a JSON list of rows, for example:\\\\n<answer>[[0, 1, 2], [3, 4, 5]]</answer>\\\\n"\\n        "Put nothing else inside the tags."\\n    )\\n    return "\\\\n".join(parts)\\n\\n\\ndef build_review_prompt(inputs, previous_candidate=None):\\n    """Build an unanchored second solve from the demonstrations alone.\\n\\n    The argument is retained for compatibility with callers, but deliberately is not\\n    included in the prompt: showing a plausible-looking first guess can make the model\\n    repeat it instead of producing a useful alternative.\\n    """\\n    return (\\n        build_prompt(inputs)\\n        + "\\\\n\\\\n## Independent second attempt\\\\n"\\n        + "Solve this puzzle from scratch using only the training input/output pairs and "\\n        + "the test input above. Check output dimensions, boundaries, and "\\n        + "unchanged cells. Return your best complete grid inside <answer></answer> tags, "\\n        + "with valid JSON rows."\\n    )\\n\\n\\ndef _valid(g):\\n    return (isinstance(g, list) and len(g) > 0 and all(isinstance(r, list) and len(r) > 0 for r in g)\\n            and len({len(r) for r in g}) == 1\\n            and all(isinstance(v, int) and not isinstance(v, bool) and 0 <= v <= 9 for r in g for v in r))\\n\\n\\ndef _parse_block(s):\\n    s = s.strip()\\n    try:\\n        g = json.loads(s)\\n        if _valid(g):\\n            return g\\n    except Exception:\\n        pass\\n    # rows of digits, optionally space/comma separated\\n    rows = []\\n    for line in s.splitlines():\\n        line = line.strip().strip("[](),")\\n        if not line:\\n            continue\\n        toks = re.findall(r"\\\\d", line) if re.fullmatch(r"[\\\\d\\\\s,]+", line) else None\\n        if not toks:\\n            return None\\n        rows.append([int(x) for x in toks])\\n    return rows if _valid(rows) else None\\n\\n\\ndef _dihedral_grids(grid):\\n    """Return the four rotations of a grid and of its horizontal reflection."""\\n    variants = []\\n    for base in (grid, [row[::-1] for row in grid]):\\n        current = [list(row) for row in base]\\n        for _ in range(4):\\n            variants.append(current)\\n            # One clockwise quarter-turn; works for rectangular grids too.\\n            current = [list(row) for row in zip(*current[::-1])]\\n    return variants\\n\\n\\ndef _infer_geometric_solution(inputs):\\n    """Infer an unambiguous D4 transform plus global color remapping from demonstrations.\\n\\n    This deliberately handles only a narrow, exactly verifiable class of rules. A\\n    hypothesis is considered only when it reproduces every demonstration cell-for-cell.\\n    If multiple hypotheses imply different test grids, defer to the model instead of\\n    arbitrarily choosing one.\\n    """\\n    train = inputs.get("train") or []\\n    test_grid = inputs.get("test_input")\\n    if not train or not _valid(test_grid):\\n        return None\\n\\n    possible_outputs = set()\\n\\n    for orientation_index in range(8):\\n        color_map = {}\\n        hypothesis_valid = True\\n        for pair in train:\\n            source = pair.get("input")\\n            target = pair.get("output")\\n            if not _valid(source) or not _valid(target):\\n                hypothesis_valid = False\\n                break\\n            oriented = _dihedral_grids(source)[orientation_index]\\n            if len(oriented) != len(target) or len(oriented[0]) != len(target[0]):\\n                hypothesis_valid = False\\n                break\\n            for source_row, target_row in zip(oriented, target):\\n                for source_color, target_color in zip(source_row, target_row):\\n                    previous = color_map.setdefault(source_color, target_color)\\n                    if previous != target_color:\\n                        hypothesis_valid = False\\n                        break\\n                if not hypothesis_valid:\\n                    break\\n            if not hypothesis_valid:\\n                break\\n        if not hypothesis_valid:\\n            continue\\n\\n        # A color absent from all demonstrations has no observed mapping. and evaluation type (for example, arc_eval).\\n    Malformed files and reports without accuracy scores are ignored so an incomplete\\n    history cannot prevent an improvement run.\\n    """\\n    if not eval_path or not os.path.isdir(eval_path):\\n        return "No evaluation reports were found (evaluation directory is unavailable)."\\n\\n    reports = []\\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(eval_path):\\n        dirs.sort()\\n        if "report.json" not in files:\\n            continue\\n        relative_parts = os.path.relpath(root, eval_path).split(os.sep)\\n        generation = None\\n        generation_index = None\\n        for index, part in enumerate(relative_parts):\\n            match = re.fullmatch(r"gen_(initial|\\\\d+)(?:\\\\..*)?", part)\\n            if match:\\n                label = match.group(1)\\n                generation = -1 if label == "initial" else int(label)\\n                generation_index = index\\n                break\\n        if generation is None:\\n            continue\\n        try:\\n            with open(os.path.join(root, "report.json"), encoding="utf-8") as stream:\\n                data = json.load(stream)\\n        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\\n            continue\\n        if not isinstance(data, dict):\\n            continue\\n        score = data.get("overall_accuracy", data.get("accuracy"))\\n        try:\\n            score = float(score)\\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\\n            continue\\n        kind_parts = relative_parts[generation_index + 1:]\\n        kind = "/".join(kind_parts) if kind_parts else "report"\\n        task_scores = data.get("per_task_score", {})\\n        failed = sorted(\\n            str(task) for task, value in task_scores.items()\\n            if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value < 1\\n        ) if isinstance(task_scores, dict) else []\\n        reports.append((generation, label, kind, score, data.get("total"), failed))\\n\\n    if not reports:\\n        return "No usable evaluation reports were found in the supplied history."\\n\\n    reports.sort(key=lambda row: (row[0], row[2]))\\n    lines = ["Prior evaluation results (accuracy; lower-scoring generations are useful regression evidence):"]\\n    # Keep the newest records while maintaining chronological order.\\n    for _, generation_label, kind, score, total, failed in reports[-max_reports:]:\\n        count = f", {total} tasks" if isinstance(total, int) else ""\\n        failures = f"; partial/failed task IDs: {\\\', \\\'.join(failed[:12])}" if failed else "; no partial/failed tasks reported"\\n        lines.append(f"- gen_{generation_label} [{kind}]: {score:.3f}{count}{failures}")\\n\\n    by_kind = {}\\n    for report in reports:\\n        by_kind.setdefault(report[2], []).append(report)\\n    for kind, entries in sorted(by_kind.items()):\\n        if len(entries) < 2:\\n            continue\\n        best = max(entries, key=lambda row: row[3])\\n        latest = entries[-1]\\n        if latest[3] < best[3]:\\n            lines.append(\\n                f"- Regression signal for {kind}: latest gen_{latest[1]} is {latest[3]:.3f}, "\\n regressions."\\n    )\\n    return "\\\\n".join(lines)\\n\\nclass MetaAgent(AgentSystem):\\n    def forward(self, repo_path, eval_path, iterations_left=None):\\n        """\\n        A meta agent that recursively self-improves.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            repo_path (str): The path to the repository.\\n            eval_path (str): The path to previously generated agents and their evaluation new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(instruction, model=self.model, msg_history=[], logging=self.log, tools_available=\\\'all\\\')\\n--- task agent ---\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport re\\nimport socket\\nimport http.client\\nimport time\\n\\n# Starting task agent (A_0) for the ARC-AGI-2 domain: one direct model response per attempt.\\n# forward() makes two independent plain attempts (the budget is 2 model calls and 2 answers per\\n# test input) and returns both parsed grids.\\n\\n\\nclass _UnixConn(http.client.HTTPConnection):\\n    def __init__(self, path, timeout=3600):\\n        super().__init__("localhost", timeout=timeout)\\n        self._path = path\\n\\n    def connect(self):\\n        s = socket.socket(socket.AF_UNIX, socket.SOCK_STREAM)\\n        s.settimeout(self.timeout)\\n        s.connect(self._path)\\n        self.sock = s\\n\\n\\ndef call_model(prompt):\\n    """One model call through the budget gateway (model and reasoning effort are fixed there)."""\\n    path = os.environ.get("GW_SOCKET", "/run/arcgw/gw.sock")\\n    body = json.dumps({"token": os.environ.get("GW_TOKEN", ""),\\n                       "messages": [{"role": "user", "content": prompt}]}).encode()\\n    last = None\\n    for attempt in range(3):\\n        try:\\n            c = _UnixConn(path)\\n            c.request("POST", "/v1/chat", body=body, headers={"Content-Type": "application/json"})\\n            r = c.getresponse()\\n            data = json.loads(r.read() or b"{}")\\n            c.close()\\n            if r.status == 200:\\n Exception as e:\\n            last = f"{type(e).__name__}: {e}"\\n        time.sleep(5 * (attempt + 1))\\n    raise RuntimeError(last)\\n\\n\\ndef grid_to_text(grid):\\n    return "\\\\n".join(" A\\n    hypothesis is considered only when it reproduces every demonstration cell-for-cell.\\n    If multiple hypotheses imply different test grids, defer to the model instead of\\n    arbitrarily choosing one.\\n    """\\n    train = inputs.get("train") or []\\n    test_grid = inputs.get("test_input")\\n    if not train or not _valid(test_grid):\\n        return None\\n\\n    possible_outputs = set()\\n\\n    for orientation_index in range(8):\\n        color_map = {}\\n        hypothesis_valid = True\\n        for pair in train:\\n            source = pair.get("input")\\n            target = pair.get("output")\\n            if not _valid(source) or not _valid(target):\\n                hypothesis_valid = False\\n                break\\n            oriented = _dihedral_grids(source)[orientation_index]\\n            if len(oriented) != len(target) or len(oriented[0]) != len(target[0]):\\n                hypothesis_valid = False\\n                break\\n            for source_row, target_row in zip(oriented, target):\\n                for source_color, target_color in zip(source_row, target_row):\\n                    previous = color_map.setdefault(source_color, target_color)\\n                    if previous != target_color:\\n                        hypothesis_valid = False\\n                        break\\n                if not hypothesis_valid:\\n                    break\\n            if not hypothesis_valid:\\n                break\\n        if not hypothesis_valid:\\n            continue\\n\\n        # A color absent from all demonstrations has no observed mapping. cannot prevent an improvement run.\\n    """\\n    if not eval_path or not os.path.isdir(eval_path):\\n        return "No evaluation reports were found (evaluation directory is unavailable)."\\n\\n    reports = []\\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(eval_path):\\n        dirs.sort()\\n        if "report.json" not in files:\\n            continue\\n        relative_parts = os.path.relpath(root, eval_path).split(os.sep)\\n        generation = None\\n        generation_index = None\\n        for index, part in enumerate(relative_parts):\\n            match = re.fullmatch(r"gen_(initial|\\\\d+)(?:\\\\..*)?", part)\\n            if match:\\n                label = match.group(1)\\n                generation = -1 if label == "initial" else int(label)\\n                generation_index = index\\n                break\\n        if generation is None:\\n            continue\\n        try:\\n            with open(os.path.join(root, "report.json"), encoding="utf-8") as stream:\\n                data = json.load(stream)\\n        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\\n            continue\\n        if not isinstance(data, dict):\\n            continue\\n        score = data.get("overall_accuracy", data.get("accuracy"))\\n        try:\\n            score = float(score)\\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\\n            continue\\n        kind_parts = relative_parts[generation_index + 1:]\\n        kind = "/".join(kind_parts) if kind_parts else "report"\\n        task_scores = data.get("per_task_score", {})\\n        failed = sorted(\\n            str(task) for task, value in task_scores.items()\\n            if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value < 1\\n        ) if isinstance(task_scores, dict) else []\\n        reports.append((generation, label, kind, score, data.get("total"), failed))\\n\\n    if not reports:\\n        return "No usable evaluation reports were found in the supplied history."\\n\\n    reports.sort(key=lambda row: (row[0], row[2]))\\n    lines = ["Prior evaluation results (accuracy; lower-scoring generations are useful regression evidence):"]\\n    # Keep the newest records while maintaining chronological order.\\n    for _, generation_label, kind, score, total, failed in reports[-max_reports:]:\\n        count = f", {total} tasks" if isinstance(total, int) else ""\\n        failures = f"; partial/failed task IDs: {\\\', \\\'.join(failed[:12])}" if failed else "; no partial/failed tasks reported"\\n        lines.append(f"- gen_{generation_label} [{kind}]: {score:.3f}{count}{failures}")\\n\\n    by_kind = {}\\n    for report in reports:\\n        by_kind.setdefault(report[2], []).append(report)\\n    for kind, entries in sorted(by_kind.items()):\\n        if len(entries) < 2:\\n            continue\\n        best = max(entries, key=lambda row: row[3])\\n        latest = entries[-1]\\n        if latest[3] < best[3]:\\n            lines.append(\\n                f"- Regression signal for {kind}: latest gen_{latest[1]} is {latest[3]:.3f}, "\\n                f"below gen_{best[1]} best {best[3]:.3f}; inspect its changes before proposing another."\\n            )\\n\\n    lines.append(\\n        "Use these reports and the associated predictions/patches to identify repeatable "\\n        "improvements, investigate recurring failures, and preserve changes that improve "\\n        "overall performance without causing avoidable regressions."\\n    )\\n    return "\\\\n".join(lines)\\n\\nclass MetaAgent(AgentSystem):\\n    def forward(self, repo_path, eval_path, iterations_left=None):\\n        """\\n        A meta agent that recursively self-improves.\\n\\n        Args:\\n            repo_path (str): The path to the repository.\\n            eval_path (str): The path to previously generated agents and their evaluation results.\\n            iterations_left (int, optional): The number of remaining iterations in which the meta agent will be invoked in future. Defaults to None.\\n        """\\n        instruction = f"Modify any part of the codebase at `{repo_path}`."\\n        # [experiment adaptation, see DECISIONS.md] gpt-6-luna otherwise asks for clarification and never edits.\\n        evaluation_summary = summarize_evaluation_history(eval_path)\\n        instruction += (\\n            f"\\\\n\\\\nThe repository\\\'s README.md describes the goal. Previously generated agents and their evaluation results are at `{eval_path}`. "\\n            "Inspect relevant prior patches and predictions as well as the reports; do not assume that a single score change proves a technique is generally better. "\\n            "Use this compact report summary to guide that inspection:\\\\n\\\\n"\\n            f"{evaluation_summary}\\\\n\\\\n"\\n            "Work autonomously: nobody will answer questions, so do not ask for clarification. "\\n            "Use the tools to inspect the code and make your changes; a response without a tool call ends the session, so only reply without one when you are done."\\n        )\\n\\n        new_msg_history = chat_with_agent(instruction, model=self.model, msg_history=[], logging=self.log, tools_available=\\\'all\\\')\\n--- generation loop ---\\n# Copyright (c) Meta Platforms, Inc. and affiliates.\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nfrom concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor\\nfrom datetime import datetime\\n\\nimport docker\\nfrom analysis.plot_progress import plot_progress_single, plot_progress_together\\nfrom analysis.visualize_archive import (\\n    visualize_archive_single,\\n    visualize_archive_together,\\n)\\n\\nfrom utils.common import file_exist_and_not_empty, load_json_file\\nfrom utils.constants import REPO_NAME\\nfrom utils.docker_utils import (\\n    build_container,\\n    cleanup_container,\\n    copy_from_container,\\n    copy_to_container,\\n    log_container_output,\\n    safe_log,\\n    setup_logger,\\n)\\nfrom utils.domain_utils import (\\n    can_domain_ensembled,\\n    get_domain_eval_subset,\\n    get_domain_splits,\\n    get_domain_stagedeval_samples,\\n)\\nfrom utils.gl_utils import (\\n    apply_diffs_container,\\n    get_patch_files,\\n    get_score,\\n    load_archive_data,\\n    run_commands_to_check_compilation,\\n    select_parent,\\n    setup_initial_gen,\\n    update_and_save_archive,\\n    update_node_metadata,\\n    get_latest_can_select_parent,\\n    is_starting_node,\\n    process_meta_patch_files,\\n)\\n\\n\\ndef run_harness_polyglot(root_dir, output_dir, genid, skip_staged_eval=False, num_samples=-1):\\n    # NOTE: the harness for polyglot is different because each task instance needs a docker container\\n    from domains.polyglot.harness import harness as harness_polyglot\\n    from domains.polyglot.report import report as report_polyglot\\n\\n    eval_output_dir = os.path.join(output_dir, f"gen_{genid}", "polyglot_eval")\\n    test_more_threshold = 0.4  # NOTE: same setting as that in DGM\\n    model_name_or_path = "eval_run"\\n    patch_files = get_patch_files(output_dir, genid)\\n    run_next_eval = True\\n\\n    # Small sample size evaluation for staged eval\\n    if not skip_staged_eval:\\n        test_task_list = load_json_file("./domains/polyglot/subsets/small.json")\\n        dnames = harness_polyglot(\\n            test_task_list=test_task_list,\\n            num_samples=-1,\\n            max_workers=10,\\n            model_name_or_path=model_name_or_path,\\n            model_patch_paths=patch_files,\\n            num_evals=1,\\n            num_evals_parallel=1,\\n            pred_dname=eval_output_dir,\\n            output_dir=eval_output_dir,\\n            root_dir=root_dir,\\n        )\\n        report_polyglot(output_dir=eval_output_dir, run_keyword=model_name_or_path, expected_num_tasks=len(test_task_list))\\n        stagedeval_score = get_score("polyglot", output_dir, genid)\\n        run_next_eval = stagedeval_score is not None and stagedeval_score >= test_more_threshold\\n\\n    # Check if additional evaluation should be run\\n    if run_next_eval:\\n        test_task_list_more = load_json_file("./domains/polyglot/subsets/medium.json")\\n        dnames = harness_polyglot(\\n            test_task_list=test_task_list + test_task_list_more,\\n            num_samples=num_samples,\\n            max_workers=10,\\n            model_name_or_path=model_name_or_path,\\n            model_patch_paths=patch_files,\\n            num_evals=1,\\n            num_evals_parallel=1,\\n            pred_dname=eval_output_dir,\\n            output_dir=eval_output_dir,\\n            root_dir=root_dir,\\n        )\\n        report_polyglot(output_dir=eval_output_dir, run_keyword=model_name_or_path, expected_num_tasks=len(test_task_list + test_task_list_more))\\n\\n    # Update metadata\\n    update_node_metadata(output_dir, genid, {"run_full_eval": run_next_eval})\\n\\ndef select_next_parent_container(\\n    docker_client,\\n    domains,\\n    generate_output_dir,\\n    archive,\\n    root_dir="./",\\n    root_commit="HEAD",\\n    max_attempts=10,\\n):\\n    # Setup logger\\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(generate_output_dir, "select_next_parent.log"))\\n\\n    # Get the latest node that can select parent\\n    latest_node = get_latest_can_select_parent(archive, generate_output_dir)\\n    safe_log(f"select_next_parent_container: latest_node={latest_node}")\\n    prev_patch_files = get_patch_files(generate_output_dir, latest_node)\\n\\n    # Create and start the Docker container\\n    image_name = f"{REPO_NAME}"\\n    run_id = datetime.now().strftime("%Y%m%d_%H%M%S_%f")\\n    container_name = f"{REPO_NAME}-nextparent-container-{run_id}"\\n    container = build_container(docker_client, root_dir, image_name, container_name, verbose=False)\\n    container.start()\\n    container_output_folder = "/tmp/"\\n\\n    try:\\n        # Apply all lineage diffs\\n        commit_hash = apply_diffs_container(container, prev_patch_files, verbose=False)\\n\\n        # Copy generate_output_dir to container\\n        container_generate_output_dir = os.path.join(\\n            container_output_folder, generate_output_dir.split(os.sep)[-1]\\n        )\\n        copy_to_container(\\n            container,\\n            source_path=generate_output_dir,\\n            dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\\n            verbose=False,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Get next parent in container\\n        command = [\\n            "timeout",\\n            "3600",  # 1h timeout\\n            "python",\\n            "-m",\\n            "utils.run_select_next_parent",\\n            "--domains",\\n            *domains,\\n            "--generate_output_dir",\\n            container_generate_output_dir,\\n        ]\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(cmd=command, workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}")\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=True)\\n\\n        # Get next parent outputs\\n        container_output_strings = exec_result.output.decode().strip().split("\\\\n")\\n        next_parent_genid = container_output_strings[-1]\\n        next_parent_genid = int(next_parent_genid) if not is_starting_node(next_parent_genid) else next_parent_genid\\n\\n    except Exception as e:\\n        safe_log(f"Error in select_next_parent_container: {e}")\\n        update_node_metadata(generate_output_dir, latest_node, {"can_select_next_parent": False})\\n        next_parent_genid = None\\n\\n    # Even on errors or KeyboardInterrupt\\n    finally:\\n        # Reset to the root commit\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\\n            cmd=["git", "reset", "--hard", root_commit], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\\n        )\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=False)\\n        exec_result = container.exec_run(\\n            cmd=["git", "clean", "-fd"], workdir=f"/{REPO_NAME}"\\n        )\\n        log_container_output(exec_result, verbose=False)\\n\\n        # Cleanup container\\n        cleanup_container(container, verbose=False)\\n\\n    # Try again if no parent is selected\\n    if next_parent_genid is None:\\n        if max_attempts > 0:\\n            next_parent_genid = select_next_parent_container(\\n                docker_client,\\n                domains,\\n                generate_output_dir,\\n                archive,\\n                root_dir,\\n                root_commit,\\n                max_attempts=max_attempts - 1,\\n            )\\n        else:\\n            # In case of infinite recursive, but it should not happen\\n            raise Exception("Max attempts reached in select_next_parent_container")\\n\\n    return next_parent_genid\\n\\ndef get_ensemble_scores_container(\\n    docker_client,\\n    domain,\\n    generate_output_dir,\\n    gen_output_dir,\\n    root_dir="./",\\n    root_commit="HEAD",\\n    prev_patch_files=[],\\n    num_samples=-1,\\n    max_workers=5,\\n    subsets=[],\\n):\\n    # Setup logger\\n    logger = setup_logger(os.path.join(gen_output_dir, "ensemble.log"))\\n\\n    # Create and start the Docker dest_path=container_generate_output_dir,\\n        )\\n\\n        # Get ensemble scores in container\\n        scores = []\\n        for subset in subsets:\\n            command = [\\n summarize_evaluation_history(eval_path, max_reports=12):\\n    """Summarize prior generation reports for the meta-agent prompt.\\n\\n    Reports are grouped by generation and evaluation type (for example, arc_eval).\\n    Malformed files and reports without accuracy scores are ignored so an incomplete\\n    history cannot prevent an improvement run.\\n    """\\n    if not eval_path or not os.path.isdir(eval_path):\\n        return "No evaluation reports were found (evaluation directory is unavailable)."\\n\\n    reports = []\\n    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(eval_path):\\n        dirs.sort()\\n        if "report.json" not in files:\\n            continue\\n        relative_parts = os.path.relpath(root, eval_path).split(os.sep)\\n        generation = None\\n        generation_index = None\\n        for index, part in enumerate(relative_parts):\\n            match = re.fullmatch(r"gen_(initial|\\\\d+)(?:\\\\..*)?", part)\\n            if match:\\n                label = match.group(1)\\n                generation = -1 if label == "initial" else int(label)\\n                generation_index = index\\n                break\\n        if generation is None:\\n            continue\\n        try:\\n            with open(os.path.join(root, "report.json"), encoding="utf-8") as stream:\\n                data = json.load(stream)\\n        except (OSError, ValueError, TypeError):\\n            continue\\n        if not isinstance(data, dict):\\n            continue\\n        score = data.get("overall_accuracy", data.get("accuracy"))\\n        try:\\n            score = float(score)\\n        except (TypeError, ValueError):\\n            continue\\n        kind_parts = relative_parts[generation_index + 1:]\\n        kind = "/".join(kind_parts) if kind_parts else "report"\\n        task_scores = data.get("per_task_score", {})\\n        failed = sorted(\\n            str(task) for task, value in task_scores.items()\\n            if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and value < 1\\n        ) if isinstance(task_scores, dict) else []\\n        reports.append((generation, label, kind, score, data.get("total"), failed))\\n\\n    if not reports:\\n        return "No usable evaluation reports were found in the supplied history."\\n\\n    reports.sort(key=lambda row: (row[0], row[2]))\\n    lines = ["Prior evaluation results (accuracy; row[3])\\n        latest = entries[-1]\\n        if latest[3] < best[3]:\\n            lines.append(\\n                f"- Regression signal for {kind}: latest gen_{latest[1]} is {latest[3]:.3f}, "\\n
